</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 495 \div 3 \\\\
&= \dfrac {495} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 165} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 165} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
&= 165
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
